# Burgenland provincial law (Landesrecht Burgenland), change by change

Part of [lexifina/global-law](https://github.com/lexifina/global-law) and **KLex**: this is Burgenland provincial law (`AT-1`), read at `austria/burgenland/acts/`.

Each amending law is one commit, credited to that law (`LGBl. Nr. 40/2024`) and dated the day it took effect, at noon in Vienna. The text is the consolidated law published by the Federal Chancellery in the [RIS](https://www.ris.bka.gv.at) (Rechtsinformationssystem des Bundes).

*Deutsch: [unten](#deutsch).*

## The questions, as git commands

| Question | Command |
| --- | --- |
| What did ALSV § 1 say on 2026-11-05? | `python tools/stand_am.py 2026-11-05 alsv par-0001.md` |
| The whole Bgld. AWH-VO 2025 as it stood on 2026-11-05 | `python tools/stand_am.py 2026-11-05 awh-vo-2025` |
| Which law wrote each paragraph of `awh-vo-2025/par-0001.md`? | `git blame awh-vo-2025/par-0001.md` |
| Every change to `awh-vo-2025/par-0001.md`, with the law and the day | `git log --follow --format="%an %(trailers:key=In-force,valueonly,separator=)" awh-vo-2025/par-0001.md` |
| Everything one law changed | `git show --stat bgbl/1994/504` |
| The laws, with the days they took effect | [`gesetze.csv`](gesetze.csv) |
| Word by word, what a law changed in a file | `git log -p --word-diff -n 2 awh-vo-2025/par-0001.md` |

## The dates, and which one we use

**Commits are dated the day the change took effect** (`In-force:`), at noon in Vienna (CET, or CEST in summer time). Days before 1970 are kept in `In-force:`; git cannot store them as timestamps, so those commits receive technical timestamps starting at `1970-01-01T00:00:01Z`, seconds apart, in order. Changes published in the Gazette but not yet in force are on the branch `future`, never on `main`.

## What "author" means

The author of a commit is the law that made the change, such as `LGBl. Nr. 40/2024`, at an address ending `@ris.invalid`, so `git blame` names the law.

## How it is checked

`tools/check_repo.py` reads every version of every provision back from git on the day it took effect, and the version before on the day before, and compares both with the text in RIS: file by file, and word for word with all the words of the RIS document read without the build's parser. `tools/test_repo.py` checks that the commands above work. The results are in [`REPRODUCE.md`](REPRODUCE.md).

## Deutsch

Burgenländisches Landesrecht (`LrKons`, ISO `AT-1`) als Git-Repository (KLex / `lexifina/global-law`-Standard).

- **Jeder Commit ist ein Änderungsgesetz**, als Autor eingetragen (`LGBl. Nr. 40/2024`), sodass `git blame` zeigt, welches Gesetz welchen Absatz geschrieben hat.
- **Jeder Commit trägt den Tag des Inkrafttretens** (`In-force:`), 12 Uhr in Wien.
- **Jeder Paragraph, Artikel und jede Anlage ist eine Datei**, jeder Absatz eine Zeile.
- Noch nicht in Kraft getretene Änderungen stehen im Zweig `future`.

Quelle: Rechtsinformationssystem des Bundes (RIS), Bundeskanzleramt, https://www.ris.bka.gv.at, lizenziert unter CC BY 4.0.
